## Bankruptcy and Insolvency Act {#bankruptcy-and-insolvency-act .Title-of-Act}

[R.S.C.]{.abbr title="Revised Statutes of Canada"}, 1985, c. B-3
:::

An Act respecting bankruptcy and insolvency
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-24355 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Short title

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Act may be cited as
the [Bankruptcy and Insolvency Act](/eng/acts/B-3).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 1
- 1992, c. 27, s. 2
:::

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-24360 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** In this Act,

[[affidavit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[affidavit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes statutory declaration and
    solemn affirmation; ([affidavit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[aircraft objects]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[aircraft objects]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2012, c. 31, s.
    414\]]{.Repealed}

[[application]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[application]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to a bankruptcy
    application filed in a court in the Province of Quebec, means a
    motion; ([Version anglaise seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

[[assignment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[assignment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an assignment filed with
    the official receiver; ([cession]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} every bank and every authorized foreign bank
      within the meaning of section 2 of the [Bank
      Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01),

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} every other member of the Canadian Payments
      Association established by the [Canadian Payments
      Act](/eng/acts/C-21), and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} every local cooperative credit society, as
      defined in subsection 2(1) of the Act referred to in paragraph
      (b), that is a member of a central cooperative credit society, as
      defined in that subsection, that is a member of that
      Association; ([banque]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bankrupt]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bankrupt]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who has made an
    assignment or against whom a bankruptcy order has been made or the
    legal status of that person; ([failli]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bankruptcy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bankruptcy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the state of being bankrupt
    or the fact of becoming bankrupt; ([faillite]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[bargaining agent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bargaining agent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any trade union that
    has entered into a collective agreement on behalf of the employees
    of a person; ([agent négociateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[child]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[child]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2000, c. 12, s.
    8\]]{.Repealed}

[[claim provable in bankruptcy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[claim provable in bankruptcy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[provable
    claim]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[claim
    provable]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any claim or liability
    provable in proceedings under this Act by a creditor; ([réclamation
    prouvable en matière de faillite]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
    [réclamation prouvable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[collective agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[collective agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to an
    insolvent person, means a collective agreement within the meaning of
    the jurisdiction governing collective bargaining between the
    insolvent person and a bargaining agent; ([convention
    collective]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[common-law partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[common-law partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to an
    individual, means a person who is cohabiting with the individual in
    a conjugal relationship, having so cohabited for a period of at
    least one year; ([conjoint de fait]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[common-law partnership]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[common-law partnership]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    relationship between two persons who are common-law partners of each
    other; ([union de fait]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a company or legal person
    that is incorporated by or under an Act of Parliament or of the
    legislature of a province, an incorporated company, wherever
    incorporated, that is authorized to carry on business in Canada or
    has an office or property in Canada or an income trust, but does not
    include banks, authorized foreign banks within the meaning of
    section 2 of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01), insurance companies,
    trust companies or loan companies; ([personne
    morale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[court]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[court]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, except in paragraphs 178(1)(a) and
    (a.1) and sections 204.1 to 204.3, means a court referred to in
    subsection 183(1) or (1.1) or a judge of that court, and includes a
    registrar when exercising the powers of the court conferred on a
    registrar under this Act; ([tribunal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[creditor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[creditor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person having a claim
    provable as a claim under this Act; ([créancier]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[current assets]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[current assets]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means cash, cash equivalents
    --- including negotiable instruments and demand deposits ---
    inventory or accounts receivable, or the proceeds from any dealing
    with those assets; ([actif à court terme]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[date of the bankruptcy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[date of the bankruptcy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a
    person, means the date of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the granting of a bankruptcy order against the
      person,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the filing of an assignment in respect of the
      person, or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the event that causes an assignment by the person
      to be deemed; ([date de la faillite]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[date of the initial bankruptcy event]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[date of the initial bankruptcy event]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in
    respect of a person, means the earliest of the day on which any one
    of the following is made, filed or commenced, as the case may be:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an assignment by or in respect of the person,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a proposal by or in respect of the person,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a notice of intention by the person,

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the first application for a bankruptcy order
      against the person, in any case

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} referred to in paragraph 50.4(8)(a) or 57(a) or
        subsection 61(2), or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in which a notice of intention to make a
        proposal has been filed under section 50.4 or a proposal has
        been filed under section 62 in respect of the person and the
        person files an assignment before the court has approved the
        proposal,

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the application in respect of which a bankruptcy
      order is made, in the case of an application other than one
      referred to in paragraph (d), or

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} proceedings under the [Companies' Creditors
      Arrangement Act](/eng/acts/C-36); ([ouverture de la
      faillite]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[debtor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[debtor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes an insolvent person and any
    person who, at the time an act of bankruptcy was committed by him,
    resided or carried on business in Canada and, where the context
    requires, includes a bankrupt; ([débiteur]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect of a corporation other
    than an income trust, means a person occupying the position of
    director by whatever name called and, in the case of an income
    trust, a person occupying the position of trustee by whatever name
    called; ([administrateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[eligible financial contract]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[eligible financial contract]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
    agreement of a prescribed kind; ([contrat financier
    admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[equity claim]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[equity claim]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a claim that is in
    respect of an equity interest, including a claim for, among others,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a dividend or similar payment,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a return of capital,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a redemption or retraction obligation,

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a monetary loss resulting from the ownership,
      purchase or sale of an equity interest or from the rescission, or,
      in Quebec, the annulment, of a purchase or sale of an equity
      interest, or

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} contribution or indemnity in respect of a claim
      referred to in any of paragraphs (a) to (d); ([réclamation
      relative à des capitaux propres]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[equity interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[equity interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a corporation other than an income
      trust, a share in the corporation --- or a warrant or option or
      another right to acquire a share in the corporation --- other than
      one that is derived from a convertible debt, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an income trust, a unit in the
      income trust --- or a warrant or option or another right to
      acquire a unit in the income trust --- other than one that is
      derived from a convertible debt; ([intérêt relatif à des capitaux
      propres]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[executing officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[executing officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a sheriff, a
    bailiff and any officer charged with the execution of a writ or
    other process under this Act or any other Act or proceeding with
    respect to any property of a debtor; ([huissier-
    exécutant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[financial collateral]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[financial collateral]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any of the
    following that is subject to an interest, or in the Province of
    Quebec a right, that secures payment or performance of an obligation
    in respect of an eligible financial contract or that is subject to a
    title transfer credit support agreement:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cash or cash equivalents, including negotiable
      instruments and demand deposits,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} securities, a securities account, a securities
      entitlement or a right to acquire securities, or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a futures agreement or a futures
      account; ([garantie financière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[General Rules]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[General Rules]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the General Rules
    referred to in section 209; ([Règles générales]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[income trust]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[income trust]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trust that has assets
    in Canada if

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its units are listed on a prescribed stock
      exchange on the date of the initial bankruptcy event, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the majority of its units are held by a trust
      whose units are listed on a prescribed stock exchange on the date
      of the initial bankruptcy event; ([fiducie de
      revenu]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[insolvent person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[insolvent person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is not
    bankrupt and who resides, carries on business or has property in
    Canada, whose liabilities to creditors provable as claims under this
    Act amount to one thousand dollars, and

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} who is for any reason unable to meet his
      obligations as they generally become due,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} who has ceased paying his current obligations in
      the ordinary course of business as they generally become due, or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the aggregate of whose property is not, at a fair
      valuation, sufficient, or, if disposed of at a fairly conducted
      sale under legal process, would not be sufficient to enable
      payment of all his obligations, due and accruing due; ([personne
      insolvable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[legal counsel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[legal counsel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any person qualified, in
    accordance with the laws of a province, to give legal
    advice; ([conseiller juridique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[locality of a debtor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[locality of a debtor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the principal
    place

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where the debtor has carried on business during
      the year immediately preceding the date of the initial bankruptcy
      event,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the debtor has resided during the year
      immediately preceding the date of the initial bankruptcy event, or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in cases not coming within paragraph (a) or (b),
      where the greater portion of the property of the debtor is
      situated; ([localité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of
    Industry; ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[net termination value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[net termination value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the net amount
    obtained after netting or setting off or compensating the mutual
    obligations between the parties to an eligible financial contract in
    accordance with its provisions; ([valeurs nettes dues à la date de
    résiliation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[official receiver]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[official receiver]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an officer appointed
    under subsection 12(2); ([séquestre officiel]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a partnership, an
    unincorporated association, a corporation, a cooperative society or
    a cooperative organization, the successors of a partnership, of an
    association, of a corporation, of a society or of an organization
    and the heirs, executors, liquidators of the succession,
    administrators or other legal representatives of a
    person; ([personne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the form of a document that is by
      this Act to be prescribed and the information to be given therein,
      means prescribed by directive issued by the Superintendent under
      paragraph 5(4)(e), and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, means prescribed by the
      General Rules; ([prescrit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[property]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[property]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any type of property, whether
    situated in Canada or elsewhere, and includes money, goods, things
    in action, land and every description of property, whether real or
    personal, legal or equitable, as well as obligations, easements and
    every description of estate, interest and profit, present or future,
    vested or contingent, in, arising out of or incident to
    property; ([bien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[proposal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[proposal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in any provision of Division I of Part III, a
      proposal made under that Division, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other provision, a proposal made under
      Division I of Part III or a consumer proposal made under Division
      II of Part III

    and includes a proposal or consumer proposal, as the case may be,
    for a composition, for an extension of time or for a scheme or
    arrangement; ([proposition concordataire]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"} ou [proposition]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[public utility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[public utility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a person or body who
    supplies fuel, water or electricity, or supplies telecommunications,
    garbage collection, pollution control or postal
    services; ([entreprise de service public]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[ordinary
    resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a resolution carried in the
    manner provided by section 115; ([résolution]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"} ou [résolution ordinaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[secured creditor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[secured creditor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person holding a
    mortgage, hypothec, pledge, charge or lien on or against the
    property of the debtor or any part of that property as security for
    a debt due or accruing due to the person from the debtor, or a
    person whose claim is based on, or secured by, a negotiable
    instrument held as collateral security and on which the debtor is
    only indirectly or secondarily liable, and includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person who has a right of retention or a prior
      claim constituting a real right, within the meaning of the Civil
      Code of Québec or any other statute of the Province of Quebec, on
      or against the property of the debtor or any part of that
      property, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any of

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the vendor of any property sold to the debtor
        under a conditional or instalment sale,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser of any property from the debtor
        subject to a right of redemption, or

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the trustee of a trust constituted by the
        debtor to secure the performance of an obligation,

      if the exercise of the person's rights is subject to the
      provisions of Book Six of the Civil Code of Québec entitled Prior
      Claims and Hypothecs that deal with the exercise of hypothecary
      rights; ([créancier garanti]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[settlement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[settlement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2005, c. 47, s.
    2\]]{.Repealed}

[[shareholder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[shareholder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a member of a
    corporation --- and, in the case of an income trust, a holder of a
    unit in an income trust --- to which this Act
    applies; ([actionnaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[sheriff]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[sheriff]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2004, c. 25, s.
    7\]]{.Repealed}

[[special resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[special resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a resolution
    decided by a majority in number and three-fourths in value of the
    creditors with proven claims present, personally or by proxy, at a
    meeting of creditors and voting on the resolution; ([résolution
    spéciale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Superintendent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Superintendent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Superintendent of
    Bankruptcy appointed under subsection
    5(1); ([surintendant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Superintendent of Financial Institutions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Superintendent of Financial
    Institutions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Superintendent of
    Financial Institutions appointed under subsection 5(1) of the
    [Office of the Superintendent of Financial Institutions
    Act](/eng/acts/O-2.7); ([surintendant des institutions
    financières]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[time of the bankruptcy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[time of the bankruptcy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a
    person, means the time of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the granting of a bankruptcy order against the
      person,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the filing of an assignment by or in respect of
      the person, or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the event that causes an assignment by the person
      to be deemed; ([moment de la faillite]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[title transfer credit support agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[title transfer credit support
    agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an agreement under which an
    insolvent person or a bankrupt has provided title to property for
    the purpose of securing the payment or performance of an obligation
    of the insolvent person or bankrupt in respect of an eligible
    financial contract; ([accord de transfert de titres pour obtention
    de crédit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[transfer at undervalue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[transfer at undervalue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a disposition
    of property or provision of services for which no consideration is
    received by the debtor or for which the consideration received by
    the debtor is conspicuously less than the fair market value of the
    consideration given by the debtor; ([opération
    sous-évaluée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[trustee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[trustee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[licensed
    trustee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is licensed or
    appointed under this Act. ([syndic]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
    [syndic autorisé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 2
- R.S., 1985, c. 31 (1st Supp.), s. 69
- 1992, c. 1, s. 145(F), c. 27, s. 3

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation of beneficiary

**[[2.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.1 .sectionLabel}** A change in the
designation of a beneficiary in an insurance contract is deemed to be a
disposition of property for the purpose of this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 2
- 2004, c. 25, s. 8
- 2005, c. 47, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.1-20041215.html "Link to previous version of section 2.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Superintendent's division office

**[[2.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.2 .sectionLabel}** Any notification,
document or other information that is required by this Act to be given,
forwarded, mailed, sent or otherwise provided to the Superintendent,
other than an application for a licence under subsection 13(1), shall be
given, forwarded, mailed, sent or otherwise provided to the
Superintendent at the Superintendent's division office as specified in
directives of the Superintendent.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 2
:::

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2005, c. 47,
s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this
  section,

  [[entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person other than an
      individual; ([entité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[related group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[related group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a group of persons
      each member of which is related to every other member of the
      group; ([groupe lié]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[unrelated group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[unrelated group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a group of persons
      that is not a related group. ([groupe non lié]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

- Definition of [[related persons]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, persons are related to
  each other and are [[related persons]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} if they are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} individuals connected by blood relationship,
    marriage, common-law partnership or adoption;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an entity and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a person who controls the entity, if it is
      controlled by one person,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a person who is a member of a related group that
      controls the entity, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any person connected in the manner set out in
      paragraph (a) to a person described in subparagraph (i) or (ii);
      or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} two entities

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} both controlled by the same person or group of
      persons,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} each of which is controlled by one person and
      the person who controls one of the entities is related to the
      person who controls the other entity,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} one of which is controlled by one person and
      that person is related to any member of a related group that
      controls the other entity,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} one of which is controlled by one person and
      that person is related to each member of an unrelated group that
      controls the other entity,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} one of which is controlled by a related group a
      member of which is related to each member of an unrelated group
      that controls the other entity, or

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} one of which is controlled by an unrelated group
      each member of which is related to at least one member of an
      unrelated group that controls the other entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Relationships

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if two entities are related to the same entity
    within the meaning of subsection (2), they are deemed to be related
    to each other;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a related group is in a position to control an
    entity, it is deemed to be a related group that controls the entity
    whether or not it is part of a larger group by whom the entity is in
    fact controlled;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a person who has a right under a contract, in
    equity or otherwise, either immediately or in the future and either
    absolutely or contingently, to, or to acquire, ownership interests,
    however designated, in an entity, or to control the voting rights in
    an entity, is, except when the contract provides that the right is
    not exercisable until the death of an individual designated in the
    contract, deemed to have the same position in relation to the
    control of the entity as if the person owned the ownership
    interests;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if a person has ownership interests in two or more
    entities, the person is, as holder of any ownership interest in one
    of the entities, deemed to be related to himself or herself as
    holder of any ownership interest in each of the other entities;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} persons are connected by blood relationship if one
    is the child or other descendant of the other or one is the brother
    or sister of the other;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} persons are connected by marriage if one is married
    to the other or to a person who is connected by blood relationship
    or adoption to the other;

  - [(f.1)]{.lawlabel} persons are connected by common-law partnership
    if one is in a common-law partnership with the other or with a
    person who is connected by blood relationship or adoption to the
    other; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} persons are connected by adoption if one has been
    adopted, either legally or in fact, as the child of the other or as
    the child of a person who is connected by blood relationship,
    otherwise than as a brother or sister, to the other.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Question of fact

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} It is a question of fact whether persons not related
  to one another were at a particular time dealing with each other at
  arm's length.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presumptions

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Persons who are related to each other are deemed not
  to deal with each other at arm's length while so related. For the
  purpose of paragraph 95(1)(b) or 96(1)(b), the persons are, in the
  absence of evidence to the contrary, deemed not to deal with each
  other at arm's length.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 4
- 2000, c. 12, s. 9
- 2004, c. 25, s. 9(F)
- 2005, c. 47, s. 5
- 2007, c. 36, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-4-20041215.html "Link to previous version of section 4"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Her Majesty]{.HTitleText1} {#h-24507 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Binding on Her Majesty

**[[4.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4.1 .sectionLabel}** This Act is binding on
Her Majesty in right of Canada or a province.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 4
:::

### [Duty of Good Faith]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1204921 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Good faith

- **[[4.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  interested person in any proceedings under this Act shall act in good
  faith with respect to those proceedings.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Good faith --- powers of court

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the court is satisfied that an interested person
  fails to act in good faith, on application by any interested person,
  the court may make any order that it considers appropriate in the
  circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 29, s. 133](#1155876-1204928){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1155876-1204928.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [PART I]{.HLabel1}[Administrative Officials]{.HTitleText1} {#h-24512 .Part}

### [Superintendent]{.HTitleText2} {#h-24513 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council shall appoint a Superintendent of Bankruptcy to
  hold office during good behaviour for a term of not more than five
  years, but the Superintendent may be removed from office by the
  Governor in Council for cause. The Superintendent's term may be
  renewed for one or more further terms.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Salary

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall be paid the salary that
  the Governor in Council may fix.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extent of supervision

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall supervise the administration
  of all estates and matters to which this Act applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall, without limiting the
  authority conferred by subsection (2),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} receive applications for licences to act as
    trustees under this Act and issue licences to persons whose
    applications have been approved;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} monitor the conditions that led to a trustee being
    issued a licence to determine whether those conditions continue to
    exist after the licence has been issued and take the appropriate
    action if he or she determines that the conditions no longer exist;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} where not otherwise provided for, require the
    deposit of one or more continuing guaranty bonds or continuing
    suretyships as security for the due accounting of all property
    received by trustees and for the due and faithful performance by
    them of their duties in the administration of estates to which they
    are appointed, in any amount that the Superintendent may determine,
    which amount may be increased or decreased as the Superintendent may
    deem expedient, and the security shall be in a form satisfactory to
    the Superintendent and may be enforced by the Superintendent for the
    benefit of the creditors;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1992, c. 27, s. 5\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} from time to time, make or cause to be made any
    inquiry or investigation of estates or other matters to which this
    Act applies, including the conduct of a trustee or a trustee acting
    as a receiver, within the meaning of subsection 243(2), or as an
    interim receiver, that the Superintendent considers appropriate, and
    for the purpose of the inquiry or investigation the Superintendent
    or any person appointed by the Superintendent for the purpose shall
    have access to and the right to examine and make copies of all
    books, records, data, including data in electronic form, documents
    and papers, that are relevant to an inquiry or investigation
    pertaining or relating to any estate or other matter to which this
    Act applies;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} receive and keep a record of all complaints from
    any creditor or other person interested in any estate and make such
    specific investigations with regard to such complaints as the
    Superintendent may determine; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} examine trustees' accounts of receipts and
    disbursements and final statements.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of Superintendent

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} intervene in any matter or proceeding in court,
    where the Superintendent considers it expedient to do so, as if the
    Superintendent were a party thereto;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} issue, to official receivers, trustees,
    administrators of consumer proposals made under Division II of Part
    III and persons who provide counselling pursuant to this Act,
    directives with respect to the administration of this Act and,
    without restricting the generality of the foregoing, directives
    requiring them

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to keep such records as the Superintendent may
      require, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to provide the Superintendent with such
      information as the Superintendent may require;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} issue such directives as may be necessary to give
    effect to any decision made by the Superintendent pursuant to this
    Act or to facilitate the carrying out of the purposes and provisions
    of this Act and the General Rules, including, without limiting the
    generality of the foregoing, directives relating to the powers,
    duties and functions of trustees, of receivers and of administrators
    as defined in section 66.11;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} issue directives governing the criteria to be
    applied by the Superintendent in determining whether a trustee
    licence is to be issued to a person and governing the qualifications
    and activities of trustees;

  - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} issue directives respecting the rules governing
    hearings for the purposes of section 14.02; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} issue directives prescribing the form of any
    document that is by this Act to be prescribed and the information to
    be given therein.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance with directives

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Every person to whom a directive is issued by the
  Superintendent under paragraph (4)(b) or (c) shall comply with the
  directive in the manner and within the time specified therein.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directives

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A directive issued by the Superintendent under this
  section shall be deemed not to be a statutory instrument within the
  meaning and for the purposes of the Statutory Instruments Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 5
- 1992, c. 27, s. 5
- 1997, c. 12, s. 4
- 2001, c. 4, s. 26(E)
- 2005, c. 47, s. 6
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-5-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Outside investigations

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Superintendent may engage any persons that the Superintendent
  considers advisable to conduct any inquiry or investigation or to take
  any other necessary action outside of the office of the
  Superintendent, and the cost and expenses of those persons shall, when
  certified by the Superintendent, be payable out of the appropriation
  for the office of the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Superintendent may examine bank account

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent, or any one duly authorized by him
  in writing on his behalf, is entitled to have access to and to examine
  and make copies of the banking accounts of a trustee in which estate
  funds may have been deposited, and, when required, all deposit slips,
  cancelled cheques or other documents relating thereto in the custody
  of the bank or the trustee shall be produced for examination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Superintendent may examine records and
  documents

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent, or anyone duly authorized in
  writing by or on behalf of the Superintendent, may with the leave of
  the court granted on an [ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} application
  examine the books, records, documents and deposit accounts of a
  trustee or any other person designated in the order granting that
  leave for the purpose of tracing or discovering the property or funds
  of an estate when there are reasonable grounds to believe or suspect
  that the property or funds of an estate have not been properly
  disclosed or dealt with and for that purpose may under a warrant from
  the court enter on and search any premises.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court order re payments from accounts

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where the Superintendent, on [ex parte]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} application, satisfies the court that it is necessary and
  in the public interest to do so, the court may issue an order
  directing a deposit-taking institution that holds a deposit account of
  a trustee or such other person as is designated in the order not to
  make payments out of the account until such time as the court
  otherwise directs.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 6
- 1997, c. 12, s. 5
- 2005, c. 47, s. 7(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 6"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[7 and 8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7_and_8 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
1992, c. 27, s. 6\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment of employees

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** Such employees as are
required to assist the Superintendent to perform his functions under
this Act shall be appointed in accordance with the [Public Service
Employment Act](/eng/acts/P-33.01).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. B-3, s. 5
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Investigations or inquiries by
Superintendent

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, on
  information supplied by an official receiver, trustee or other person,
  the Superintendent suspects, on reasonable grounds, that a person has,
  in connection with any estate or matter to which this Act applies,
  committed an offence under this or any other Act of Parliament, the
  Superintendent may, if it appears to the Superintendent that the
  alleged offence might not otherwise be investigated, make or cause to
  be made any inquiries or investigations that the Superintendent
  considers appropriate.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1992, c. 27, s. 7\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Examination

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, on the application of the Superintendent or the
  Superintendent's authorized representative, a subpoena has been issued
  by the court, the Superintendent may, for the purpose of an inquiry or
  investigation under subsection (1), examine or cause to be examined
  under oath before the registrar of the court or other authorized
  person, the trustee, the debtor, any person who the Superintendent
  suspects, on reasonable grounds, has knowledge of the affairs of the
  debtor, or any person who is or has been an agent or a mandatary, or a
  clerk, a servant, an officer, a director or an employee of the debtor
  or the trustee, with respect to the conduct, dealings and transactions
  of the debtor, the causes of the bankruptcy or insolvency of the
  debtor, the disposition of the debtor's property or the administration
  of the estate, and may order any person liable to be so examined to
  produce any books, records, data, including data in electronic form,
  documents or papers in the person's possession or under the person's
  control.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Questions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person being examined pursuant to this section is
  bound to answer all questions relating to the conduct, dealings and
  transactions of the debtor, the causes of the debtor's bankruptcy or
  insolvency and the disposition of the debtor's property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Privilege of witness

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where a person being examined pursuant to this
  section objects to answering any question on the ground that his
  answer may tend to criminate him or may tend to establish his
  liability to a civil proceeding at the instance of the Crown or of any
  person and if, but for this section or section 5 of the [Canada
  Evidence Act](/eng/acts/C-5), he would have been excused from
  answering that question, the answer so given shall not be used or
  admitted in evidence against him in any proceeding, civil or criminal,
  thereafter taking place other than a prosecution for perjury in the
  giving of that evidence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} No person shall hinder, molest or interfere with any
  person doing anything that he is authorized by or pursuant to this
  section to do, or prevent or attempt to prevent any person doing any
  such thing, and, notwithstanding any other Act or law, every person
  shall, unless he is unable to do so, do everything he is required by
  or pursuant to this section to do.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Where any book, record, paper or other document is
  examined or produced in accordance with this section, the person by
  whom it is examined or to whom it is produced or the Superintendent
  may make or cause to be made one or more copies thereof, and a
  document purporting to be certified by the Superintendent or a person
  thereunto authorized by him to be a copy made pursuant to this section
  is admissible in evidence and has the same probative force as the
  original document would have if it were proven in the ordinary way.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 10
- 1992, c. 27, s. 7
- 2004, c. 25, s. 10
- 2005, c. 47, s. 8
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10-20041215.html "Link to previous version of section 10"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reporting offence to provincial authority

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  after an investigation pursuant to section 10 or otherwise the
  Superintendent has obtained evidence of an offence having been
  committed in connection with an estate or matter to which this Act
  applies, the Superintendent shall report the alleged offence to the
  deputy attorney general of the province concerned or to such person as
  is duly designated by that deputy attorney general for that purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Costs and expenses

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding section 136, a recovery made as the
  result of any inquiries or investigation made or caused to be made
  pursuant to section 10 shall be applied to the reimbursement of any
  costs and expenses incurred by the Superintendent thereon, not being
  ordinary costs or expenses of the office of the Superintendent, and
  the balance thereafter remaining in respect of the recovery shall be
  made available for the benefit of the creditors of the debtor.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 11
- 1992, c. 27, s. 8
- 2004, c. 25, s. 11(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Public Records]{.HTitleText2} {#h-24585 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public records

- **[[11.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall keep, or
  cause to be kept, in such form as the Superintendent deems appropriate
  and for the prescribed period, a public record of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} proposals,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} bankruptcies,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} licences issued to trustees by the Superintendent,
    and appointments or designations of administrators made by the
    Superintendent, and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} notices sent to the Superintendent by receivers
    pursuant to subsection 245(1)

  and, on request therefor and on payment of such fee as may be
  prescribed, shall provide, or cause to be provided, any information
  contained in that public record.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other records

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall keep, or cause to be kept,
  in such form as the Superintendent deems appropriate and for the
  prescribed period, such other records relating to the administration
  of this Act as the Superintendent deems advisable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreement to provide compilation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may enter into an agreement to
  provide a compilation of all or part of the information that is
  contained in the public record.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 8
- 2007, c. 36, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.1-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 11.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Official Receivers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-24600 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Bankruptcy districts and divisions

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Each
  of the provinces constitutes one bankruptcy district for the purposes
  of this Act but the Governor in Council may divide any bankruptcy
  district into two or more bankruptcy divisions and name or number
  them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Official receivers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council shall appoint one or more
  official receivers in each bankruptcy division who shall be deemed to
  be officers of the court and shall have and perform the duties and
  responsibilities specified by this Act and the General Rules.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report to Superintendent

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The official receiver shall make a report to the
  Superintendent, in the prescribed form, of every bankruptcy
  originating in his division, and he shall also notify the
  Superintendent of any subsequent increase or decrease in the security
  filed by the trustee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registrar to act for official receiver

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In the absence or illness of the official receiver or
  pending the appointment of a successor when the office is vacant, the
  registrar of the court shall perform the duties of the official
  receiver.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. B-3, s. 8
:::

### [Trustees]{.HTitleText2} {#h-24612 .Subheading}

#### [Licensing of Trustees]{.HTitleText3} {#h-24613 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for licence

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who wishes to obtain a licence to act as a trustee shall file
  with the Superintendent an application for a licence in the prescribed
  form.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions of eligibility

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent, after such investigation
  concerning an applicant for a licence to act as a trustee as the
  Superintendent considers necessary, may issue the licence if the
  Superintendent is satisfied, having regard to the criteria referred to
  in paragraph 5(4)(d), that the applicant is qualified to obtain the
  licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-eligibility

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may refuse to issue a licence to
  an applicant who is insolvent or has been found guilty of an
  indictable offence that, in the Superintendent's opinion, is of a
  character that would impair the trustee's capacity to perform his or
  her fiduciary duties.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 13
- 1992, c. 27, s. 9
- 1997, c. 12, s. 6
- 2005, c. 47, s. 9
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 13"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of licence

**[[13.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13.1 .sectionLabel}** A licence shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} be in the prescribed form;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} specify the bankruptcy district or part thereof in
  which the trustee is entitled to act; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} be subject to such conditions and limitations as the
  Superintendent considers appropriate and may specify therein.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 9
- 1997, c. 12, s. 7
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees payable

- **[[13.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Prior to the issue of a licence, the
  applicant shall pay such fees as may be prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual fees

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each year, starting with the year in which a licence
  is issued to a trustee, the trustee shall pay the prescribed fees on
  or before the prescribed date or, if there is no prescribed date, on
  or before December 31.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When licence invalid

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A licence ceases to be valid on the failure of the
  trustee to pay a fee in accordance with subsection (2) or if the
  trustee becomes bankrupt.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Superintendent may reinstate licence

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a licence has ceased to be valid by reason of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} failure to pay fees, the Superintendent may
    reinstate it where the trustee pays the outstanding fees together
    with a prescribed penalty amount and provides a reasonable written
    explanation of the failure to pay them in accordance with subsection
    (2); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the trustee becoming bankrupt, the Superintendent
    may, on written representations made by the trustee, reinstate the
    licence subject to such conditions and limitations as the
    Superintendent considers appropriate and may specify therein.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension or cancellation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A licence may be suspended or cancelled by the
  Superintendent

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the trustee has been found guilty of an
    indictable offence that, in the Superintendent's opinion, is of a
    character that would impair the trustee's capacity to perform his or
    her fiduciary duties;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the trustee has failed to comply with any of the
    conditions or limitations to which the licence is subject;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the trustee has ceased to act as a trustee; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} at the request of the trustee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of intended decision

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Notice of an intended decision under subsection (5)
  shall be in writing setting out the Superintendent's reasons therefor
  and shall be sent to the trustee at least ten days before the decision
  takes effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a licence ceases to be valid by virtue of
  subsection (3) or is suspended or cancelled under subsection (5), the
  Superintendent may impose on the trustee any requirements that the
  Superintendent considers appropriate, including a requirement that the
  trustee provide security for the protection of an estate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of procedure

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, section 14.02 does not apply
  in respect of a suspension or cancellation of a licence under
  subsection (5).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 9
- 1997, c. 12, s. 8
- 2004, c. 25, s. 12
- 2005, c. 47, s. 10
- [2019, c. 29, s. 160](#1156019-1170778){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1156019-1170778.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous

:::

#### [Conduct of Trustees]{.HTitleText3} {#h-24655 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where trustee is not qualified to act

- **[[13.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except with the permission of the
  court and on such conditions as the court may impose, no trustee shall
  act as trustee in relation to the estate of a debtor

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where the trustee is, or at any time during the two
    preceding years was,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a director or officer of the debtor,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an employer or employee of the debtor or of a
      director or officer of the debtor,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} related to the debtor or to any director or
      officer of the debtor, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the auditor, accountant or legal counsel, or a
      partner or an employee of the auditor, accountant or legal
      counsel, of the debtor; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the trustee is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the trustee under a trust indenture issued by the
      debtor or any person related to the debtor, or the holder of a
      power of attorney under an act constituting a hypothec within the
      meaning of the Civil Code of Québec that is granted by the debtor
      or any person related to the debtor, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} related to the trustee, or the holder of a power
      of attorney, referred to in subparagraph (i).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of application to Superintendent

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A trustee who applies for the permission of the
  court for the purposes of subsection (1) shall without delay send a
  copy of the application to the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where disclosure required

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No trustee shall act as a trustee in relation to the
  estate of a debtor where the trustee is already

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the trustee in the bankruptcy of, or in a proposal
    concerning, any person related to the debtor, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the receiver, within the meaning of subsection
    243(2), or the liquidator of the property of any person related to
    the debtor,

  without making, at the time of being appointed as trustee in relation
  to the estate of the debtor and at the first meeting of creditors,
  full disclosure of that fact and of the potential conflict of
  interest.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 9
- 1997, c. 12, s. 9(F)
- 2004, c. 25, s. 13
- 2005, c. 47, s. 11
- 2007, c. 36, s. 4(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13.3-20041215.html "Link to previous version of section 13.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Trustee may act for secured creditor

- **[[13.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13.4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  trustee may, while acting as the trustee of an estate, act for or
  assist a secured creditor to assert a claim against the estate or to
  realize or otherwise deal with a security that the secured creditor
  holds, unless the trustee has obtained a written opinion from
  independent legal counsel that the security is valid and enforceable
  against the estate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification by trustee

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Forthwith on commencing to act for or assist a
  secured creditor of the estate in the manner set out in subsection
  (1), a trustee shall notify the Superintendent and the creditors or
  the inspectors

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that the trustee is acting for the secured
    creditor;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} of the basis of any remuneration from the secured
    creditor; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} of the opinion referred to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Trustee to provide opinion

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Within two days after receiving a request therefor, a
  trustee shall provide the Superintendent with a copy of the opinion
  referred to in subsection (1) and shall also provide a copy to each
  creditor who has made a request therefor.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 9
- 1997, c. 12, s. 10
- 2004, c. 25, s. 14(E)
- 2005, c. 47, s. 12
- 2007, c. 36, s. 5
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13.4-20041215.html "Link to previous version of section 13.4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Code of Ethics

**[[13.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13.5 .sectionLabel}** A trustee shall
comply with the prescribed Code of Ethics.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 9
- 2005, c. 47, s. 13
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13.5-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 13.5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Persons disqualified from working for
trustee

**[[13.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13.6 .sectionLabel}** A trustee shall not
engage the services of a person

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} whose trustee licence has been cancelled under
  paragraph 13.2(5)(a) or subsection 14.01(1); or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} who is the subject of a direction made by the
  Superintendent under paragraph 14.03(1)(d).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 11
- 2005, c. 47, s. 13
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13.6-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 13.6"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Appointment and Substitution of Trustees]{.HTitleText3} {#h-24698 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment of trustee by creditors

**[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** The creditors may, at any
meeting by special resolution, appoint or substitute another licensed
trustee for the trustee named in an assignment, a bankruptcy order or a
proposal, or otherwise appointed or substituted.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 14
- 1992, c. 27, s. 9
- 2004, c. 25, s. 15
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-14-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 14"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision affecting licence

- **[[14.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, after making or causing to be
  made an inquiry or investigation into the conduct of a trustee, it
  appears to the Superintendent that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a trustee has not properly performed the duties of
    a trustee or has been guilty of any improper management of an
    estate,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a trustee has not fully complied with this Act, the
    General Rules, directives of the Superintendent or any law with
    regard to the proper administration of any estate, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it is in the public interest to do so,

  the Superintendent may do one or more of the following:

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} cancel or suspend the licence of the trustee;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} place such conditions or limitations on the licence
    as the Superintendent considers appropriate including a requirement
    that the trustee successfully take an exam or enrol in a proficiency
    course;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} require the trustee to make restitution to the
    estate of such amount of money as the estate has been deprived of as
    a result of the trustee's conduct; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} require the trustee to do anything that the
    Superintendent considers appropriate and that the trustee has agreed
    to.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to former trustees

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} This section and section 14.02 apply, in so far as
  they are applicable, in respect of former trustees, with such
  modifications as the circumstances require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may delegate by written
  instrument, on such terms and conditions as are therein specified, any
  or all of the Superintendent's powers, duties and functions under
  subsection (1), subsection 13.2(5), (6) or (7) or section 14.02 or
  14.03.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification to trustees

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the Superintendent delegates in accordance with
  subsection (2), the Superintendent or the delegate shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where there is a delegation in relation to trustees
    generally, give written notice of the delegation to all trustees;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} whether or not paragraph (a) applies, give written
    notice of the delegation of a power to any trustee who may be
    affected by the exercise of that power, either before the power is
    exercised or at the time the power is exercised.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 9
- 1997, c. 12, s. 12
- 2005, c. 47, s. 14
- 2007, c. 36, s. 6
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-14.01-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 14.01"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to trustee

- **[[14.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14.02
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before deciding whether to exercise
  any of the powers referred to in subsection 14.01(1), the
  Superintendent shall send the trustee written notice of the powers
  that the Superintendent may exercise and the reasons why they may be
  exercised and afford the trustee a reasonable opportunity for a
  hearing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Summons

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may, for the purpose of the
  hearing, issue a summons requiring and commanding any person named in
  it

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to appear at the time and place mentioned in it;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to testify to all matters within their knowledge
    relative to the subject matter of the inquiry or investigation into
    the conduct of the trustee; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to bring and produce any books, records, data,
    documents or papers --- including those in electronic form --- in
    their possession or under their control relative to the subject
    matter of the inquiry or investigation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect throughout Canada

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} A person may be summoned from any part of Canada by
  virtue of a summons issued under subsection (1.1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees and allowances

  [(1.3)]{.lawlabel} Any person summoned under subsection (1.1) is
  entitled to receive the like fees and allowances for so doing as if
  summoned to attend before the Federal Court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Procedure at hearing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} At a hearing referred to in subsection (1), the
  Superintendent

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has the power to administer oaths;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not bound by any legal or technical rules of
    evidence in conducting the hearing;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} shall deal with the matters set out in the notice
    of the hearing as informally and expeditiously as the circumstances
    and a consideration of fairness permit; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} shall cause a summary of any oral evidence to be
    made in writing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The notice referred to in subsection (1) and, where
  applicable, the summary of oral evidence referred to in paragraph
  (2)(d), together with such documentary evidence as the Superintendent
  receives in evidence, form the record of the hearing and the record
  and the hearing are public, unless the Superintendent is satisfied
  that personal or other matters that may be disclosed are of such a
  nature that the desirability of avoiding public disclosure of those
  matters, in the interest of a third party or in the public interest,
  outweighs the desirability of the access by the public to information
  about those matters.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The decision of the Superintendent after a hearing
  referred to in subsection (1), together with the reasons therefor,
  shall be given in writing to the trustee not later than three months
  after the conclusion of the hearing, and is public.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review by Federal Court

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A decision of the Superintendent given pursuant to
  subsection (4) is deemed to be a decision of a federal board,
  commission or other tribunal that may be reviewed and set aside
  pursuant to the [Federal Courts Act](/eng/acts/F-7).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 9
- 1997, c. 12, s. 13
- 2002, c. 8, s. 182
- 2005, c. 47, s. 15
- 2007, c. 36, s. 7
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-14.02-20030702.html "Link to previous version of section 14.02"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conservatory measures

- **[[14.03]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14.03
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  Superintendent may, for the protection of an estate, the rights of the
  creditors or the debtor,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} direct a person to deal with property of the estate
    described in the direction in such manner as may be indicated in the
    direction, including the continuation of the administration of the
    estate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} direct any person to take such steps as the
    Superintendent considers necessary to preserve the books, records,
    data, including data in electronic form, and documents of the
    estate;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} direct a bank or other depository not to pay out
    funds held to the credit of the estate except in accordance with the
    direction; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} direct the official receiver not to appoint the
    trustee in respect of any new estates until a decision is made under
    subsection 13.2(5) or 14.01(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Circumstances

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The circumstances in which the Superintendent is
  authorized to exercise the powers set out in subsection (1) are where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an estate is left without a trustee by the death,
    removal or incapacity of the trustee;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Superintendent makes or causes to be made any
    inquiry or investigation under paragraph 5(3)(e);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Superintendent exercises any of the powers set
    out in section 14.01;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the fees referred to in subsection 13.2(2) have not
    been paid in respect of the trustee's licence;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a trustee becomes insolvent;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a trustee has been found guilty of an indictable
    offence that, in the Superintendent's opinion, is of a character
    that would impair the trustee's capacity to perform the trustee's
    fiduciary duties, or has failed to comply with any of the conditions
    or limitations to which the trustee's licence is subject; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a circumstance referred to in paragraph 13.2(5)(c)
    or (d) exists and the Superintendent is considering cancelling the
    licence under subsection 13.2(5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents and effect of direction

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A direction given pursuant to subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shall state the statutory authority pursuant to
    which the direction is given;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is binding on the person to whom it is given; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is, in favour of the person to whom it is given,
    conclusive proof of the facts set out therein.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability ceases on compliance

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person who complies with a direction given pursuant
  to subsection (1) is not liable for any act done by the person only to
  comply with the direction.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 9
- 1997, c. 12, s. 14
- 1999, c. 31, s. 18(E)
- 2005, c. 47, s. 16
- 2007, c. 36, s. 8(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-14.03-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 14.03"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal and appointment

**[[14.04]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14.04 .sectionLabel}** The court, on the
application of any interested person, may for cause remove a trustee and
appoint another licensed trustee in the trustee's place.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 9
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where there is no licensed trustee, etc.

**[[14.05]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14.05 .sectionLabel}** Where a debtor
resides or carries on business in a locality in which there is no
licensed trustee, and no licensed trustee can be found who is willing to
act as trustee, the court or the official receiver may appoint a
responsible person residing in the locality of the debtor to administer
the estate of the debtor, and that person, for that purpose, has all the
powers of a licensed trustee under this Act, and the provisions of this
Act apply to that person as if a licence had been issued to that person
under paragraph 5(3)(a).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 9
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No trustee is bound to act

- **[[14.06]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14.06
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No trustee is bound to assume the
  duties of trustee in matters relating to assignments, bankruptcy
  orders or proposals, but having accepted an appointment in relation to
  those matters the trustee shall, until discharged or another trustee
  is appointed in the trustee's stead, perform the duties required of a
  trustee under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} In subsections (1.2) to (6), a reference to a
  trustee means a trustee in a bankruptcy or proposal and includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an interim receiver;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a receiver within the meaning of subsection 243(2);
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other person who has been lawfully appointed to
    take, or has lawfully taken, possession or control of any property
    of an insolvent person or a bankrupt that was acquired for, or is
    used in relation to, a business carried on by the insolvent person
    or bankrupt.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No personal liability in respect of
  matters before appointment

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} Despite anything in federal or provincial law, if a
  trustee, in that position, carries on the business of a debtor or
  continues the employment of a debtor's employees, the trustee is not
  by reason of that fact personally liable in respect of a liability,
  including one as a successor employer,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is in respect of the employees or former
    employees of the debtor or a predecessor of the debtor or in respect
    of a pension plan for the benefit of those employees; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that exists before the trustee is appointed or that
    is calculated by reference to a period before the appointment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Status of liability

  [(1.3)]{.lawlabel} A liability referred to in subsection (1.2) is not
  to rank as costs of administration.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability of other successor employers

  [(1.4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1.2) does not affect the liability of a
  successor employer other than the trustee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability in respect of environmental
  matters

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding anything in any federal or provincial
  law, a trustee is not personally liable in that position for any
  environmental condition that arose or environmental damage that
  occurred

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} before the trustee's appointment; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} after the trustee's appointment unless it is
    established that the condition arose or the damage occurred as a
    result of the trustee's gross negligence or wilful misconduct or, in
    the Province of Quebec, the trustee's gross or intentional fault.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reports, etc., still required

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in subsection (2) exempts a trustee from any
  duty to report or make disclosure imposed by a law referred to in that
  subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-liability re certain orders

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding anything in any federal or provincial
  law but subject to subsection (2), where an order is made which has
  the effect of requiring a trustee to remedy any environmental
  condition or environmental damage affecting property involved in a
  bankruptcy, proposal or receivership, the trustee is not personally
  liable for failure to comply with the order, and is not personally
  liable for any costs that are or would be incurred by any person in
  carrying out the terms of the order,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if, within such time as is specified in the order,
    within ten days after the order is made if no time is so specified,
    within ten days after the appointment of the trustee, if the order
    is in effect when the trustee is appointed, or during the period of
    the stay referred to in paragraph (b), the trustee

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} complies with the order, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} on notice to the person who issued the order,
      abandons, disposes of or otherwise releases any interest in any
      real property, or any right in any immovable, affected by the
      condition or damage;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} during the period of a stay of the order granted,
    on application made within the time specified in the order referred
    to in paragraph (a), within ten days after the order is made or
    within ten days after the appointment of the trustee, if the order
    is in effect when the trustee is appointed, by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the court or body having jurisdiction under the
      law pursuant to which the order was made to enable the trustee to
      contest the order, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the court having jurisdiction in bankruptcy for
      the purposes of assessing the economic viability of complying with
      the order; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the trustee had, before the order was made,
    abandoned or renounced or been divested of any interest in any real
    property, or any right in any immovable, affected by the condition
    or damage.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stay may be granted

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The court may grant a stay of the order referred to
  in subsection (4) on such notice and for such period as the court
  deems necessary for the purpose of enabling the trustee to assess the
  economic viability of complying with the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Costs for remedying not costs of
  administration

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the trustee has abandoned or renounced any
  interest in any real property, or any right in any immovable, affected
  by the environmental condition or environmental damage, claims for
  costs of remedying the condition or damage shall not rank as costs of
  administration.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Priority of claims

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Any claim by Her Majesty in right of Canada or a
  province against the debtor in a bankruptcy, proposal or receivership
  for costs of remedying any environmental condition or environmental
  damage affecting real property or an immovable of the debtor is
  secured by security on the real property or immovable affected by the
  environmental condition or environmental damage and on any other real
  property or immovable of the debtor that is contiguous with that real
  property or immovable and that is related to the activity that caused
  the environmental condition or environmental damage, and the security

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is enforceable in accordance with the law of the
    jurisdiction in which the real property or immovable is located, in
    the same way as a mortgage, hypothec or other security on real
    property or immovables; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ranks above any other claim, right, charge or
    security against the property, despite any other provision of this
    Act or anything in any other federal or provincial law.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Claim for clean-up costs

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 121(1), a claim against a debtor
  in a bankruptcy or proposal for the costs of remedying any
  environmental condition or environmental damage affecting real
  property or an immovable of the debtor shall be a provable claim,
  whether the condition arose or the damage occurred before or after the
  date of the filing of the proposal or the date of the bankruptcy.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 9
- 1997, c. 12, s. 15
- 2004, c. 25, s. 16
- 2005, c. 47, s. 17
- 2007, c. 36, s. 9
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-14.06-20041215.html "Link to previous version of section 14.06"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of defect or irregularity in
appointment

**[[14.07]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14.07 .sectionLabel}** No defect or
irregularity in the appointment of a trustee vitiates any act done by
the trustee in good faith.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 9
:::

#### [Corporations as Trustees]{.HTitleText3} {#h-24830 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Majority of officers and directors must
hold licences

**[[14.08]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14.08 .sectionLabel}** A body corporate
may hold a licence as a trustee only if a majority of its directors and
a majority of its officers hold licences as trustees.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 9
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acts of body corporate

**[[14.09]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14.09 .sectionLabel}** A body corporate
that holds a licence as a trustee may perform the duties and exercise
the powers of a trustee only through a director or officer of the body
corporate who holds a licence as a trustee.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 9
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not carrying on business of trust company

**[[14.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14.1 .sectionLabel}** Every body corporate
that is incorporated by or under an Act of Parliament and that holds a
licence as a trustee may carry on the business of a trustee anywhere in
Canada and shall not, in respect of its operations as a trustee, be
construed to be carrying on the business of a trust company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 9
:::

#### [Official Name]{.HTitleText3} {#h-24843 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Official name in bankruptcy and proposal
proceedings

**[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** The official name of a
trustee acting in bankruptcy proceedings is "The Trustee of the Estate
of (*insert name of the bankrupt*), a bankrupt", and the official name
of a trustee acting with respect to a proposal by an insolvent person is
"The Trustee acting [in re]{.otherLang lang="la"} the proposal of
(*insert the name of the debtor*)".

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. B-3, s. 11
:::

#### [Status of Trustee]{.HTitleText3} {#h-24848 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Declaration

**[[15.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15.1 .sectionLabel}** A trustee is deemed
to be a trustee for the purposes of the definition
[[trustee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in section 2 of the [Criminal
Code](/eng/acts/C-46).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 16
- 2004, c. 25, s. 17(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-15.1-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 15.1"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Duties and Powers of Trustees]{.HTitleText3} {#h-24853 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security to be given by trustee

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  trustee duly appointed shall, as soon as they are appointed, give
  security in cash or by bond or suretyship of a guaranty company
  satisfactory to the official receiver for the due accounting for, the
  payment and the transfer of all property received by the trustee as
  trustee and for the due and faithful performance of the trustee's
  duties.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security to be given by trustee

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The security required to be given under
  subsection (1) shall be given to the official receiver in favour of
  the creditors generally and may be enforced by any succeeding trustee
  or by any one of the creditors on behalf of all by direction of the
