    terms and method of payment of the claims, or that no terms be
    presently fixed; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any case he may on notice of motion refer any
    objection to be disposed of by the court or as the court otherwise
    directs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issue of order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} After the conclusion of the hearing referred to in
  subsection (1), the clerk shall enter in the register his decision or
  the decision of the court, as the case may be, and issue a
  consolidation order.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. B-3, s. 194
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issue of consolidation order

**[[224]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-224 .sectionLabel}** Where no objection has
been received within thirty days after the date of the sending of the
notice of an application for a consolidation order pursuant to section
220, the clerk shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} make an entry in the register to that effect; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} issue the consolidation order.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 224
- 1992, c. 1, s. 18, c. 27, s. 83
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of consolidation order

- **[[225]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-225 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  consolidation order shall state the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of and the amount owing to each creditor
    named in the register; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amounts to be paid into court by the debtor and
    the times of payment thereof or, where applicable, that the present
    circumstances of the debtor do not warrant the immediate settling of
    any such amounts or times.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A consolidation order

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is a judgment of the court in favour of each
    creditor named in the register for the amount stated therein to be
    owing to that creditor;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is an order of the court for the payment by the
    debtor of the amounts stated therein and at the stated times; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} bears interest at the rate which by law applies to
    a judgment debt arising from a judgment of the court that granted
    the order or at such other rate as may be prescribed by regulation.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 225
- 1992, c. 27, s. 84
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consolidation order not to be issued

- **[[226]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-226 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  consolidation order that does not provide for the payment in full of
  all the debts to which it refers within a period of three years shall
  not be issued unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all registered creditors consent thereto in
    writing; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the order is approved by the court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed consent

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Where a registered creditor does not give any
  response to a request for consent under paragraph (1)(a) within thirty
  days after receiving such a request, the creditor shall be deemed to
  have consented to the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Referral to court

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any consolidation order referred to in subsection (1)
  shall be referred to the court for approval or otherwise by the clerk
  on notice of motion to any registered creditor who has not consented
  thereto in writing.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 226
- 1992, c. 27, s. 85
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review of consolidation order

- **[[227]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-227 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  court may, on application to review a consolidation order of the clerk
  made by notice of motion within fifteen days after the making of the
  order by any of the parties affected thereby, review the consolidation
  order and confirm or vary it or set it aside and make such disposition
  of the matter as the court sees fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision to be entered

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The clerk shall enter any decision made by the court
  under subsection (1) in the register and the decision takes effect in
  place of the order of the clerk.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 227
- 1997, c. 12, s. 115
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms may be imposed on debtor

**[[228]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-228 .sectionLabel}** The court may, in
deciding any matter brought before it, impose such terms on a debtor
with respect to the custody of his property or any disposition thereof
or of the proceeds thereof as it deems proper to protect the registered
creditors and may give such directions for that purpose as the
circumstances require.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. B-3, s. 199
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Process stayed by consolidation order

**[[229]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-229 .sectionLabel}** On the making of a
consolidation order, no process shall be issued out of any court in the
province in which the debtor resides against the debtor at the instance
of a creditor in respect of any debt to which this Part applies, except
as permitted by this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. B-3, s. 200
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assignments of debtor's property to clerk

- **[[230]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-230 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  clerk may, at any time after the making of a consolidation order,
  require of and take from the debtor an assignment to himself as clerk
  of the court of any moneys due, owing or payable or to become due,
  owing or payable to the debtor, or earned or to be earned by the
  debtor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise agreed on, the clerk shall forthwith
  notify the person owing or about to owe the moneys of the assignment
  referred to in subsection (1) and all moneys collected thereon shall
  be applied to the credit of the claims against the debtor under the
  consolidation order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Writ of execution

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The clerk may issue a writ of execution or
  certificate of judgment in respect of a consolidation order and cause
  it to be filed in any place where the writ or certificate may bind or
  be a charge on land or chattels.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. B-3, s. 201
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adding creditors after order

- **[[231]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-231
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where, at any time before the
  payment in full of the claims against a debtor under a consolidation
  order, the clerk is notified of a claim to which this Part applies
  that is not entered in the order, he shall, subject to subsection (2)
  and on notice to the debtor and creditor and to each registered
  creditor,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} settle the amount owing to the creditor;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where he deems it necessary to do so, vary the
    amounts to be paid by the debtor into court and the times of payment
    thereof in order to provide for the new claim; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} enter the matters referred to in paragraphs (a)
    and (b) in the register.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court to decide

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the debtor or any registered creditor disputes
  the claim of a creditor described in subsection (1), the clerk shall
  on notice of motion refer the matter to the court and the decision of
  the court shall be entered in the register.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The clerk shall make such amendments to the
  consolidation order as may be necessary to give effect to any entries
  in the register made pursuant to this section, and shall give notice
  thereof to the registered creditors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Creditor to share

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} On the entry of a claim in the register pursuant to
  this section, the creditor shall share with the other creditors in any
  further distribution of moneys paid into court by or on behalf of the
  debtor.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. B-3, s. 202
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Secured claims

- **[[232]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-232 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  registered creditor holding security for a claim may, at any time,
  elect to rely on his security notwithstanding that the claim is
  included in a consolidation order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proceeds in excess

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the proceeds from the disposal of the security
  referred to in subsection (1) are in excess of the registered
  creditor's claim, the excess shall be paid into court and applied in
  payment of other judgments against the debtor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply where the security is
  in the form of chattels exempt from seizure under any law in force in
  the province in which the consolidation order was issued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reduced claim

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where the proceeds from the disposal of the security
  referred to in subsection (1) are less than the registered creditor's
  claim, the creditor remains entitled to the balance of his claim.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (4) does not apply in a case where, under
  the law in force in the province in which the consolidation order was
  issued, a creditor

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} who enforces his security by repossession or
    repossession and sale, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} who seizes and sells the security under an
    execution issued pursuant to a judgment obtained against the debtor
    in respect of the claim so secured,

  is limited in his recovery of the claim to the security so repossessed
  or the proceeds of the sale thereof.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. B-3, s. 203
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Enforcement of order in default of debtor

- **[[233]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-233 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  registered creditor may apply by notice of motion to the court where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a debtor defaults in complying with any order or
    direction of the court;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other proceeding for the recovery of money is
    brought against the debtor;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the debtor has, after the consolidation order was
    made, incurred further debts totalling in excess of five hundred
    dollars;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a judgment is recovered against the debtor larger
    in amount than a judgment to which this Part applies without the
    judgment creditor's consent, and the judgment creditor refuses to
    permit his name to be added to the register; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the debtor has property or funds that should be
    made available for the satisfaction of the consolidation order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}*Ex parte* application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A registered creditor may apply [ex parte]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} to the court where a debtor

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is about to abscond or has absconded from the
    province in which the consolidation order was issued leaving
    personal property liable to seizure under execution; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with intent to defraud his creditors has attempted
    or is attempting to remove from the province in which the
    consolidation order was issued personal property liable to seizure
    under execution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proceedings authorized

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On the application referred to in subsection (1) or
  (2), the court may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} authorize the registered creditor making the
    application to take on behalf of all the registered creditors such
    proceedings to enforce the consolidation order as the court deems
    advisable; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where it deems it advisable and on notice to all
    parties, make an order permitting all the registered creditors to
    proceed each independently of the others for the enforcement of
    their claims under the consolidation order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Moneys applied to judgment

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} All moneys recovered as a result of proceedings taken
  pursuant to paragraph (3)(a) after payment of costs incurred thereby
  shall be paid into the court and shall be applied to the credit of the
  judgments against the debtor appearing in the register.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proceedings where continuing default

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where a debtor defaults in making any payment into
  court required to be made under a consolidation order and the default
  continues for a period of three months, all the registered creditors
  are entitled to proceed forthwith, each independently of the others
  and without reference to the court, for the enforcement of their
  claims under the consolidation order, unless the court otherwise
  directs on being satisfied, on application by the debtor, that the
  circumstances giving rise to the default and to its continuation were
  beyond the control of the debtor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debtor not entitled to relief

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Where any order has been made under paragraph (3)(b)
  or any proceedings have been commenced under subsection (5), the
  debtor under the consolidation order is not, without the leave of the
  court, entitled to any further relief under this Part during the
  currency of any claim against him entered in the register.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 233
- 1997, c. 12, s. 116
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Re-examination of debtor

- **[[234]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-234 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  debtor or any registered creditor may at any time apply [ex
  parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} to the clerk for a further examination
  and hearing of the debtor in respect of his financial circumstances.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The further hearing referred to in subsection (1) may
  only be held

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with the leave of the clerk; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the event of the refusal of the clerk, with
    leave of the court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of hearing

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The clerk shall give all parties to the consolidation
  order at least thirty days notice of the time appointed for the
  hearing referred to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clerk may vary order, etc.

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where after considering the evidence presented at the
  further hearing referred to in subsection (1) the clerk is of the
  opinion that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the terms of payment set out in the consolidation
    order, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the decision that the circumstances of the debtor
    do not warrant the immediate settling of any amounts or times of
    payment thereof,

  should be changed because of a change in the circumstances of the
  debtor, he may

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} vary the order with respect to the amounts to be
    paid by the debtor into court or the times of payment thereof, or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} on notice of motion refer the matter to the court
    for settlement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of section 227

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Section 227 applies, with such modifications as the
  circumstances require, to a decision of the clerk under subsection
  (4).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 234
- 1992, c. 1, s. 19
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition of moneys paid into court

- **[[235]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-235
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), the clerk
  shall distribute the moneys paid into court on account of the debts of
  a debtor at least once every three months.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The clerk shall distribute the money paid under
  subsection (1) rateably, or as nearly so as is practicable, among the
  registered creditors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payments less than five dollars

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Except in the case of a final payment under a
  consolidation order, the clerk is not required to make a payment to
  any creditor if the amount thereof is less than five dollars.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. B-3, s. 206
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Oaths

- **[[236]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-236 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  clerk may for the purposes of this Part examine any person under oath
  and may administer oaths.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The clerk shall make a written record in summary form
  of all evidence given at a hearing.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. B-3, s. 207
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If assignment or bankruptcy order made

- **[[237]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-237 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  debtor in respect of whom a consolidation order has been issued under
  this Part makes an assignment under section 49, or if a bankruptcy
  order is made against the debtor under section 43, or if a proposal by
  the debtor is approved by the court having jurisdiction in bankruptcy
  under sections 59 to 61, any moneys that have been paid into court as
  required by the consolidation order and that have not yet been
  distributed to the registered creditors shall, immediately after the
  making of the assignment or bankruptcy order or the approval of the
  proposal, be distributed among those creditors by the clerk in the
  proportions to which they are entitled under the consolidation order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proceedings may be taken under other
  Parts

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The fact that proceedings have been taken under this
  Part does not prevent the taking of proceedings by or against the
  debtor under the provisions of any other Part of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} None of the provisions of Parts I to IX of this Act
  applies to proceedings under this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 237
- 2004, c. 25, s. 96
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-237-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 237"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal

**[[238]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-238 .sectionLabel}** A decision or order of
the court under this Part is subject to appeal in the same manner as if
it were a judgment of the court in a civil action.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. B-3, s. 209
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clerk to report

- **[[239]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-239 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  the issue of any consolidation order, the clerk shall forward a copy
  thereof to the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The clerk shall give to the Superintendent all
  reports that the Superintendent may require for the administration of
  this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 239
- 1992, c. 27, s. 86
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No dismissal, etc., of employees

**[[239.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-239.1 .sectionLabel}** No employer shall
dismiss, suspend, lay off or otherwise discipline a debtor by reason
only that the debtor has applied for a consolidation order under this
Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 87
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No discontinuance of public utilities

- **[[239.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-239.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No public utility shall discontinue
  service to a debtor by reason only that the debtor

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is insolvent;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has applied for a consolidation order under this
    Part; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has not paid for service rendered before the
    consolidation order was applied for.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cash payments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in subsection (1) shall be construed as
  requiring further supply of service for other than payment in cash.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 87
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[240]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-240 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the forms to be used under this Part;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting costs, fees and levies to be paid under
  this Part;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} designating the "court" for the purpose of this Part
  in any province except Manitoba and Alberta;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} adapting this Part to the court organization or other
  circumstances of a particular province;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} varying, in respect of any province, the classes of
  debts and amounts thereof to which this Part applies;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} changing or prescribing, in respect of any province,
  the classes of debts to which this Part does not apply;

- [(f.1)]{.lawlabel} respecting the transfer of proceedings to a
  province other than the province in which a consolidation order was
  originally issued; and

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} generally, for carrying into effect the purposes and
  provisions of this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 240
- 1992, c. 27, s. 88
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Audit of proceedings

**[[241]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-241 .sectionLabel}** The accounts of every
clerk that relate to proceedings under this Part are subject to audit in
the same manner as if the accounts were the accounts of a provincial
officer.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. B-3, s. 212
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of this Part

- **[[242]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-242 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council shall, at the request of the lieutenant governor
  in council of a province, declare, by order, that this Part applies or
  ceases to apply, as the case may be, in respect of the province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Automatic application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to an order being made under subsection (1)
  declaring that this Part ceases to apply in respect of a province, if
  this Part is in force in the province immediately before that
  subsection comes into force, this Part applies in respect of the
  province.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. B-3, s. 242
- 2002, c. 7, s. 85
- 2007, c. 36, s. 57
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-242-20030401.html "Link to previous version of section 242"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART XI]{.HLabel1}[Secured Creditors and Receivers]{.HTitleText1} {#h-28565 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court may appoint receiver

- **[[243]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-243
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (1.1), on
  application by a secured creditor, a court may appoint a receiver to
  do any or all of the following if it considers it to be just or
  convenient to do so:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} take possession of all or substantially all of the
    inventory, accounts receivable or other property of an insolvent
    person or bankrupt that was acquired for or used in relation to a
    business carried on by the insolvent person or bankrupt;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} exercise any control that the court considers
    advisable over that property and over the insolvent person's or
    bankrupt's business; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} take any other action that the court considers
    advisable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on appointment of receiver

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} In the case of an insolvent person in respect of
  whose property a notice is to be sent under subsection 244(1), the
  court may not appoint a receiver under subsection (1) before the
  expiry of 10 days after the day on which the secured creditor sends
  the notice unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the insolvent person consents to an earlier
    enforcement under subsection 244(2); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the court considers it appropriate to appoint a
    receiver before then.

- Definition of [[receiver]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) and (4), in this Part,
  [[receiver]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is appointed under subsection (1); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is appointed to take or takes possession or control
    --- of all or substantially all of the inventory, accounts
    receivable or other property of an insolvent person or bankrupt that
    was acquired for or used in relation to a business carried on by the
    insolvent person or bankrupt --- under

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an agreement under which property becomes subject
      to a security (in this Part referred to as a "security
      agreement"), or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a court order made under another Act of
      Parliament, or an Act of a legislature of a province, that
      provides for or authorizes the appointment of a receiver or
      receiver-manager.

- Definition of [[receiver]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} --- subsection 248(2)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 248(2), the definition
  [[receiver]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection (2) is to be read
  without reference to paragraph (a) or subparagraph (b)(ii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Trustee to be appointed

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Only a trustee may be appointed under subsection (1)
  or under an agreement or order referred to in paragraph (2)(b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place of filing

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The application is to be filed in a court having
  jurisdiction in the judicial district of the locality of the debtor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Orders respecting fees and
  disbursements

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a receiver is appointed under subsection (1), the
  court may make any order respecting the payment of fees and
  disbursements of the receiver that it considers proper, including one
  that gives the receiver a charge, ranking ahead of any or all of the
  secured creditors, over all or part of the property of the insolvent
  person or bankrupt in respect of the receiver's claim for fees or
  disbursements, but the court may not make the order unless it is
  satisfied that the secured creditors who would be materially affected
  by the order were given reasonable notice and an opportunity to make
  representations.

- Meaning of [[disbursements]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} In subsection (6),
  [[disbursements]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} does not include payments made
  in the operation of a business of the insolvent person or bankrupt.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 89
- 2005, c. 47, s. 115
- 2007, c. 36, s. 58
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-243-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 243"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Advance notice

- **[[244]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-244 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  secured creditor who intends to enforce a security on all or
  substantially all of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the inventory,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the accounts receivable, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the other property

  of an insolvent person that was acquired for, or is used in relation
  to, a business carried on by the insolvent person shall send to that
  insolvent person, in the prescribed form and manner, a notice of that
  intention.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a notice is required to be sent under
  subsection (1), the secured creditor shall not enforce the security in
  respect of which the notice is required until the expiry of ten days
  after sending that notice, unless the insolvent person consents to an
  earlier enforcement of the security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No advance consent

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), consent to
  earlier enforcement of a security may not be obtained by a secured
  creditor prior to the sending of the notice referred to in subsection
  (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply, or ceases to apply, in
  respect of a secured creditor

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whose right to realize or otherwise deal with his
    security is protected by subsection 69.1(5) or (6); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of whom a stay under sections 69 to 69.2
    has been lifted pursuant to section 69.4.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply where there is a receiver
  in respect of the insolvent person.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 89
- 1994, c. 26, s. 9(E)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Receiver to give notice

- **[[245]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-245 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  receiver shall, as soon as possible and not later than ten days after
  becoming a receiver, by appointment or otherwise, in respect of
  property of an insolvent person or a bankrupt, send a notice of that
  fact, in the prescribed form and manner, to the Superintendent,
  accompanied by the prescribed fee, and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a bankrupt, to the trustee; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an insolvent person, to the
    insolvent person and to all creditors of the insolvent person that
    the receiver, after making reasonable efforts, has ascertained.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A receiver in respect of property of an insolvent
  person shall forthwith send notice of his becoming a receiver to any
  creditor whose name and address he ascertains after sending the notice
  referred to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Names and addresses of creditors

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An insolvent person shall, forthwith after being
  notified that there is a receiver in respect of any of his property,
  provide the receiver with the names and addresses of all creditors.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 89
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Receiver's statement

- **[[246]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-246 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  receiver shall, forthwith after taking possession or control,
  whichever occurs first, of property of an insolvent person or a
  bankrupt, prepare a statement containing the prescribed information
  relating to the receivership, and shall forthwith provide a copy
  thereof to the Superintendent and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to the insolvent person or the trustee (in the case
    of a bankrupt); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to any creditor of the insolvent person or the
    bankrupt who requests a copy at any time up to six months after the
    end of the receivership.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Receiver's interim reports

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A receiver shall, in accordance with the General
  Rules, prepare further interim reports relating to the receivership,
  and shall provide copies thereof to the Superintendent and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to the insolvent person or the trustee (in the case
    of a bankrupt); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to any creditor of the insolvent person or the
    bankrupt who requests a copy at any time up to six months after the
    end of the receivership.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Receiver's final report and statement
  of accounts

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A receiver shall, forthwith after completion of
  duties as receiver, prepare a final report and a statement of
  accounts, in the prescribed form and containing the prescribed
  information relating to the receivership, and shall forthwith provide
  a copy thereof to the Superintendent and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to the insolvent person or the trustee (in the case
    of a bankrupt); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to any creditor of the insolvent person or the
    bankrupt who requests a copy at any time up to six months after the
    end of the receivership.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 89
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Intellectual property --- sale or
disposition

- **[[246.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-246.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the insolvent person or the
  bankrupt is a party to an agreement that grants to another party a
  right to use intellectual property that is included in a sale or
  disposition by the receiver, that sale or disposition does not affect
  that other party's right to use the intellectual property ---
  including the other party's right to enforce an exclusive use ---
  during the term of the agreement, including any period for which the
  other party extends the agreement as of right, as long as the other
  party continues to perform its obligations under the agreement in
  relation to the use of the intellectual property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Intellectual property --- disclaimer or
  resiliation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the insolvent person or the bankrupt is a party to
  an agreement that grants to another party a right to use intellectual
  property, the disclaimer or resiliation of that agreement by the
  receiver does not affect that other party's right to use the
  intellectual property --- including the other party's right to enforce
  an exclusive use --- during the term of the agreement, including any
  period for which the other party extends the agreement as of right, as
  long as the other party continues to perform its obligations under the
  agreement in relation to the use of the intellectual property.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 268](#1039382-1204920){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039382-1204920.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Good faith, etc.

**[[247]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247 .sectionLabel}** A receiver shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} act honestly and in good faith; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} deal with the property of the insolvent person or the
  bankrupt in a commercially reasonable manner.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 89
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of court

- **[[248]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-248
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where the court, on the application
  of the Superintendent, the insolvent person, the trustee (in the case
  of a bankrupt), a receiver or a creditor, is satisfied that the
  secured creditor, the receiver or the insolvent person is failing or
  has failed to carry out any duty imposed by sections 244 to 247, the
  court may make an order, on such terms as it considers proper,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} directing the secured creditor, receiver or
    insolvent person, as the case may be, to carry out that duty, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} restraining the secured creditor or receiver, as
    the case may be, from realizing or otherwise dealing with the
    property of the insolvent person or bankrupt until that duty has
    been carried out,

  or both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On the application of the Superintendent, the
  insolvent person, the trustee (in the case of a bankrupt) or a
  creditor, made within six months after the statement of accounts was
  provided to the Superintendent pursuant to subsection 246(3), the
  court may order the receiver to submit the statement of accounts to
  the court for review, and the court may adjust, in such manner and to
  such extent as it considers proper, the fees and charges of the
  receiver as set out in the statement of accounts.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 89
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Receiver may apply to court for
directions

**[[249]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-249 .sectionLabel}** A receiver may apply to
the court for directions in relation to any provision of this Part, and
the court shall give, in writing, such directions, if any, as it
considers proper in the circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 89
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to apply to court

- **[[250]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-250 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application may be made under section 248 or 249 notwithstanding any
  order of a court as defined in subsection 243(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where inconsistency

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where there is any inconsistency between an order
  made under section 248, or a direction given under section 249, and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the security agreement or court order under which
    the receiver acts or was appointed, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other order of the court that appointed the
    receiver,

  the order made under section 248 or the direction given under section
  249, as the case may be, prevails to the extent of the inconsistency.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 89
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Protection of receivers

**[[251]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251 .sectionLabel}** No action lies against
a receiver for loss or damage arising from the sending or providing by
the receiver of a notice pursuant to section 245 or a statement or
report pursuant to section 246, if done in good faith in compliance or
intended compliance with those sections.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 89
- 1997, c. 12, s. 117(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Defence available

**[[252]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-252 .sectionLabel}** In any proceeding where
it is alleged that a secured creditor or a receiver contravened or
failed to comply with any provision of this Part, it is a defence if the
secured creditor or the receiver, as the case may be, shows that, at the
time of the alleged contravention or failure to comply, he had
reasonable grounds to believe that the debtor was not insolvent.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 27, s. 89
:::

## [PART XII]{.HLabel1}[Securities Firm Bankruptcies]{.HTitleText1} {#h-28677 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-28678 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[253]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-253 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,

[[customer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[customer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person with or for whom a securities firm deals
      as principal, or agent or mandatary, and who has a claim against
      the securities firm in respect of a security received, acquired or
      held by the securities firm in the ordinary course of business as
      a securities firm from or for a securities account of that person

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for safekeeping or deposit or in segregation,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} with a view to sale,

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} to cover a completed sale,

      - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} pursuant to a purchase,

      - [(v)]{.lawlabel} to secure performance of an obligation of that
        person, or

      - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of effecting a transfer,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person who has a claim against the securities
      firm arising out of a sale or wrongful conversion by the
      securities firm of a security referred to in paragraph (a), and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a person who has cash or other assets held in a
      securities account with the securities firm,

    but does not include a person who has a claim against the securities
    firm for cash or securities that, by agreement or operation of law,
    is part of the capital of the securities firm or a claim that is
    subordinated to claims of creditors of the securities
    firm; ([client]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[customer compensation body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[customer compensation body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    prescribed body and includes, unless it is prescribed to be excluded
    from this definition, the Canadian Investor Protection
    Fund; ([organisme d'indemnisation des clients]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[customer name securities]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[customer name securities]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means securities
    that on the date of bankruptcy of a securities firm are held by or
    on behalf of the securities firm for the account of a customer and
    are registered or recorded in the appropriate manner in the name of
    the customer or are in the process of being so registered or
    recorded, but does not include securities registered or recorded in
    the appropriate manner in the name of the customer that, by
    endorsement or otherwise, are negotiable by the securities
    firm; ([valeur mobilière immatriculée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[deferred customer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[deferred customer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a customer whose
    misconduct, either in the customer's capacity as a customer or
    otherwise, caused or materially contributed to the insolvency of a
    securities firm; ([client responsable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[eligible financial contract]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[eligible financial contract]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    2007, c. 29, s. 101\]]{.Repealed}

[[hold]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[hold]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a security, includes
    holding it in electronic form; ([détenir]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[net equity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[net equity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, with respect to the
    securities account or accounts of a customer, maintained in one
    capacity, the net dollar value of the account or accounts, equal to
    the amount that would be owed by a securities firm to the customer
    as a result of the liquidation by sale or purchase at the close of
    business of the securities firm on the date of bankruptcy of the
    securities firm, of all security positions of the customer in each
    securities account, other than customer name securities reclaimed by
    the customer, including any amount in respect of a securities
    transaction not settled on the date of bankruptcy but settled
    thereafter, less any indebtedness of the customer to the securities
    firm on the date of bankruptcy including any amount owing in respect
    of a securities transaction not settled on the date of bankruptcy
    but settled thereafter, plus any payment of indebtedness made with
    the consent of the trustee after the date of bankruptcy; ([capitaux
    nets]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[open contractual commitment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[open contractual commitment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
    enforceable contract of a securities firm to purchase or sell a
    security that was not completed by payment and delivery on the date
    of bankruptcy; ([contrat en cours]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[securities firm]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[securities firm]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who carries
    on the business of buying and selling securities from, to or for a
    customer, whether or not as a member of an exchange, as principal,
    or agent or mandatary, and includes any person required to be
    registered to enter into securities transactions with the public,
    but does not include a corporate entity that is not a corporation
    within the meaning of section 2; ([courtier en valeurs
    mobilières]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any document, instrument or
    written or electronic record that is commonly known as a security,
    and includes, without limiting the generality of the foregoing,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a document, instrument or written or electronic
      record evidencing a share, participation right or other right or
      interest in property or in an enterprise, including an equity
      share or stock, or a mutual fund share or unit,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a document, instrument or written or electronic
      record evidencing indebtedness, including a note, bond, debenture,
      mortgage, hypothec, certificate of deposit, commercial paper or
      mortgage-backed instrument,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a document, instrument or written or electronic
      record evidencing a right or interest in respect of an option,
      warrant or subscription, or under a commodity future, financial
      future, or exchange or other forward contract, or other derivative
      instrument, including an eligible financial contract, and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} such other document, instrument or written or
      electronic record as is prescribed. ([valeur
      mobilière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou [titre]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
- 2004, c. 25, s. 97(E)
- 2005, c. 47, s. 117
- 2007, c. 29, s. 101
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-253-20070622.html "Link to previous version of section 253"){.PITLink}
:::

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-28707 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of other provisions

- **[[254]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-254 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} All
  of the provisions of this Act apply, with such modifications as the
  circumstances require, in respect of claims by customers for
  securities and customer name securities as if customers were creditors
  in respect of such claims.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of transaction provisions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Sections 95 to 101 apply, with such modifications as
  the circumstances require, in respect of transactions of a customer
  with or through a securities firm relating to securities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} This Part does not apply to proceedings under Part
  III.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Termination, netting or setting off or
  compensation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in this Part affects the rights of a party to
  a contract, including an eligible financial contract, with respect to
  termination, netting or setting off or compensation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Secured creditors

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The operation of this Part is subject to the rights
  of secured creditors.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
- 2004, c. 25, s. 98(E)
- 2007, c. 29, s. 102
- 2017, c. 26, s. 10
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-254-20070622.html "Link to previous version of section 254"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflicts

**[[255]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-255 .sectionLabel}** All the provisions of
this Act, in so far as they are applicable, apply in respect of
bankruptcies under this Part, but if a conflict arises between the
application of the provisions of this Part and the other provisions of
this Act, the provisions of this Part prevail.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applications re securities firm

- **[[256]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-256 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  addition to any creditor who may file an application in accordance
  with sections 43 to 45, an application for a bankruptcy order against
  a securities firm may be filed by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a securities commission established under an
    enactment of a province, if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the securities firm has committed an act of
      bankruptcy referred to in section 42 or subsection (2) of this
      section within the six months before the filing of the application
      and while the securities firm was licensed or registered by the
      securities commission to carry on business in Canada, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case in which the act of bankruptcy was
      that referred to in subsection (2), the suspension referred to in
      that subsection is in effect when the application is filed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a securities exchange recognized by a provincial
    securities commission, if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the securities firm has committed an act of
      bankruptcy referred to in section 42 or subsection (2) of this
      section within the six months before the filing of the application
      and while the securities firm was a member of the securities
      exchange, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case in which the act of bankruptcy was
      that referred to in subsection (2), the suspension referred to in
      that subsection is in effect when the application is filed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a customer compensation body, if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the securities firm has committed an act of
      bankruptcy referred to in section 42 or subsection (2) of this
      section within the six months before the filing of the application
      and while the securities firm had customers whose securities
      accounts were protected, in whole or in part, by the customer
      compensation body, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case in which the act of bankruptcy was
      that referred to in subsection (2), the suspension referred to in
      that subsection is in effect when the application is filed; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a person who, in respect of property of a
    securities firm, is a receiver within the meaning of subsection
    243(2), a receiver-manager, a liquidator or any other person with
    similar functions appointed under a federal or provincial enactment
    relating to securities that provides for the appointment of that
    other person, if the securities firm has committed an act of
    bankruptcy referred to in section 42 within the six months before
    the filing of the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraphs (1)(a) to (c),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the suspension by a securities commission referred
    to in paragraph (1)(a) of a securities firm's registration to trade
    in securities, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the suspension by a securities exchange referred to
    in paragraph (1)(b) of a securities firm's membership in that
    exchange

  constitutes an act of bankruptcy if the suspension is due to the
  failure of the firm to meet capital adequacy requirements.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service on securities commission

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a securities exchange files an application under
    paragraph (1)(b), or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a customer compensation body files an application
    under paragraph (1)(c),

  a copy of the application must be served on the securities commission,
  if any, having jurisdiction in the locality of the securities firm
  where the application was filed, before

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any prescribed interval preceding the hearing of
    the application, or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any shorter interval that may be fixed by the court
    and that precedes the hearing of the application.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
- 2004, c. 25, s. 99
- 2005, c. 47, s. 118
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-256-20041215.html "Link to previous version of section 256"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement of customer account

**[[257]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-257 .sectionLabel}** The trustee of the
estate of a securities firm shall send to customers a statement of
customer accounts with the firm together with the notice under
subsection 102(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deferred customers

- **[[258]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-258
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where the trustee is of the opinion
  that a customer should be treated as a deferred customer, the trustee
  shall apply to the court for a ruling on the matter and shall send the
  customer a copy of the application, together with a statement of the
  reasons why the customer should be so treated, and the court may, on
  such notice as it considers appropriate, make such order as it
  considers appropriate in the circumstances.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application by customer compensation
  body

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where securities accounts of customers are protected
  by a customer compensation body, the customer compensation body may
  apply to the court for a ruling as to whether a customer should be
  treated as a deferred customer and, in the case of such an
  application,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the customer compensation body shall send the
    customer a copy of the application together with a statement setting
    out the reasons why the customer should be so treated; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the court may, on such notice as it considers
    appropriate, make such order as it considers appropriate in the
    circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Trustee powers

**[[259]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-259 .sectionLabel}** The trustee may, in
respect of a bankruptcy under this Part, without the permission of
inspectors until inspectors are appointed and thereafter with the
permission of inspectors,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} exercise a power of attorney in respect of and
  transfer any security vested in the trustee;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} sell securities, other than customer name securities;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} purchase securities;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} discharge any security on securities vested in the
  trustee;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} complete open contractual commitments;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} maintain customers' securities accounts and meet
  margin calls;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} distribute cash and securities to customers;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} transfer securities accounts to another securities
  firm, to the extent practicable, comply with customer requests
  regarding the disposal of open contractual commitments and the
  transfer of open contractual commitments to another securities firm,
  and enter into agreements to indemnify the other securities firm
  against shortages of cash or securities in transferred accounts;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} liquidate any securities account without notice; and

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} sell, without tender, assets of the securities firm
  essential to the carrying on of its business.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
- 2004, c. 25, s. 100
- 2015, c. 3, s. 10(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-259-20041215.html "Link to previous version of section 259"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of customer name securities

**[[260]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-260 .sectionLabel}** The trustee shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} determine which of the securities in customers'
  securities accounts are to be dealt with as customer name securities
  and those that are not to be dealt with as such; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} advise customers with securities determined to be
  customer name securities of the determination as soon as possible
  thereafter.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
:::

### [Distribution of Estate]{.HTitleText2} {#h-28785 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vesting of securities, etc., in trustee

- **[[261]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-261 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  securities firm becomes bankrupt, the following securities and cash
  vest in the trustee:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} securities owned by the securities firm;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} securities and cash held by any person for the
    account of the securities firm; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} securities and cash held by the securities firm for
    the account of a customer, other than customer name securities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Establishment of a customer pool fund
  and a general fund

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a securities firm becomes bankrupt and property
  vests in a trustee under subsection (1) or under other provisions of
  this Act, the trustee shall establish

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a fund, in this Part called the "customer pool
    fund", including therein

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} securities, including those obtained after the
      date of the bankruptcy, but excluding customer name securities and
      excluding eligible financial contracts to which the firm is a
      party, that are held by or for the account of the firm

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} for a securities account of a customer,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} for an account of a person who has entered into
        an eligible financial contract with the firm and has deposited
        the securities with the firm to assure the performance of the
        person's obligations under the contract, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} for the firm's own account,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} cash, including cash obtained after the date of
      the bankruptcy, and including

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} dividends, interest and other income in respect
        of securities referred to in subparagraph (i),

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} proceeds of disposal of securities referred to
        in subparagraph (i), and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} proceeds of policies of insurance covering
        claims of customers to securities referred to in subparagraph
        (i),

    - []{.lawlabel} that is held by or for the account of the firm

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} for a securities account of a customer,

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} for an account of a person who has entered into
        an eligible financial contract with the firm and has deposited
        the cash with the firm to assure the performance of the person's
        obligations under the contract, or

      - [(F)]{.lawlabel} for the firm's own securities account, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any investments of the securities firm in its
      subsidiaries that are not referred to in subparagraph (i) or (ii);
      and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a fund, in this Part called the "general fund",
    including therein all of the remaining vested property.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
- 2004, c. 25, s. 101(F)
- 2005, c. 47, s. 119
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-261-20041215.html "Link to previous version of section 261"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Allocation and distribution of cash and
securities in customer pool fund

- **[[262]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-262 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Cash
  and securities in the customer pool fund shall be allocated in the
  following priority:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for costs of administration referred to in
    paragraph 136(1)(b), to the extent that sufficient funds are not
    available in the general fund to pay such costs;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to customers, other than deferred customers, in
    proportion to their net equity; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to the general fund.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where property deposited with
  securities firm under an EFC

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person has, under the terms of an eligible
    financial contract with the securities firm, deposited property with
    the firm to assure the performance of the person's obligations under
    the contract, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that property is included in the customer pool fund
    pursuant to paragraph 261(2)(a),

  that person shall share in the distribution of the customer pool fund
  as if the person were a customer of the firm with a claim for net
  equity equal to the net value of the property deposited that would
  have been returnable to the person after deducting any amount owing by
  the person under the contract.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distribution

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} To the extent that securities of a particular type
  are available in the customer pool fund, the trustee shall distribute
  them to customers with claims to the securities, in proportion to
  their claims to the securities, up to the appropriate portion of their
  net equity, unless the trustee determines that, in the circumstances,
  it would be more appropriate to sell the securities and distribute the
  proceeds to the customers with claims to the securities in proportion
  to their claims to the securities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compensation in kind

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the trustee may satisfy
  all or part of a customer's claim to securities of a particular type
  by delivering to the customer securities of that type to which the
  customer was entitled at the date of bankruptcy. For greater
  certainty, the trustee may, for that purpose, exercise the trustee's
  power to purchase securities in accordance with section 259.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Allocation of property in the general
  fund

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Property in the general fund shall be allocated in
  the following priority:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to preferred creditors in the order set out in
    subsection 136(1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} rateably

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to customers, other than deferred customers,
      having claims for net equity remaining after distribution of
      property from the customer pool fund and property provided by a
      customer compensation body, where applicable, in proportion to
      claims for net equity remaining,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} where applicable, to a customer compensation
      body to the extent that it paid or compensated customers in
      respect of their net equity, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} to creditors in proportion to the values of
      their claims;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} rateably to creditors referred to in section 137;
    and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to deferred customers, in proportion to their
    claims for net equity.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
- 2005, c. 47, s. 120
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-262-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 262"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery of customer name securities

- **[[263]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-263
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where a customer is not indebted to
  a securities firm, the trustee shall deliver to the customer the
  customer name securities that belong to the customer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where customer indebted to securities
  firm

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a customer to whom customer name securities
  belong and who is indebted to the securities firm on account of
  customer name securities not fully paid for, or on another account,
  discharges their indebtedness in full, the trustee shall deliver to
  that customer the customer name securities that belong to the
  customer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Customer indebted to securities firm

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a customer to whom customer name securities belong
  and who is indebted to the securities firm on account of customer name
  securities not fully paid for, or on another account, does not
  discharge their indebtedness in full, the trustee may, on notice to
  the customer, sell sufficient customer name securities to discharge
  the indebtedness, and those securities are then free of any right,
  title or interest of the customer. If the trustee so discharges the
  customer's indebtedness, the trustee shall deliver any remaining
  customer name securities to the customer.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
- 2005, c. 47, s. 121
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-263-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 263"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Trustee to consult customer compensation
body

**[[264]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-264 .sectionLabel}** Where the accounts of
customers of a securities firm are protected, in whole or in part, by a
customer compensation body, the trustee shall consult the customer
compensation body on the administration of the bankruptcy, and the
customer compensation body may designate an inspector to act on its
behalf.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Late claims

**[[265]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-265 .sectionLabel}** A customer may prove a
claim after the distribution of cash and securities in the customer pool
fund and is entitled to receive cash and securities in the hands of the
trustee at the time the claim is proven up to the appropriate portion of
the customer's net equity before further distribution is made to other
customers, but no such claim shall affect the previous distribution of
the customer pool fund or the general fund.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
:::

### [Accounting of Trustee]{.HTitleText2} {#h-28854 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement of trustee required

**[[266]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-266 .sectionLabel}** In addition to any
other statement or report required to be prepared under this Act, a
trustee shall prepare

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the distribution of property in the customer pool
    fund among customers who have proved their claims, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the disposal of customer name securities; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} such other report relating to that distribution or
  disposal as the court may direct.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
:::

## [PART XIII]{.HLabel1}[Cross-border Insolvencies]{.HTitleText1} {#h-28863 .Part}

### [Purpose]{.HTitleText2} {#h-28864 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purpose

**[[267]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-267 .sectionLabel}** The purpose of this
Part is to provide mechanisms for dealing with cases of cross-border
insolvencies and to promote

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} cooperation between the courts and other competent
  authorities in Canada with those of foreign jurisdictions in cases of
  cross-border insolvencies;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} greater legal certainty for trade and investment;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the fair and efficient administration of cross-border
  insolvencies that protects the interests of creditors and other
  interested persons, and those of debtors;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the protection and the maximization of the value of
  debtors' property; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the rescue of financially troubled businesses to
  protect investment and preserve employment.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
- 2005, c. 47, s. 122
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-267-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 267"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-28874 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[268]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-268 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Part.

  [[foreign court]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[foreign court]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a judicial or other
      authority competent to control or supervise a foreign
      proceeding. ([tribunal étranger]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[foreign main proceeding]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[foreign main proceeding]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a foreign
      proceeding in a jurisdiction where the debtor has the centre of
      the debtor's main interests. ([principale]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[foreign non-main proceeding]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[foreign non-main proceeding]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      foreign proceeding, other than a foreign main
      proceeding. ([secondaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[foreign proceeding]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[foreign proceeding]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a judicial or an
      administrative proceeding, including an interim proceeding, in a
      jurisdiction outside Canada dealing with creditor's collective
      interests generally under any law relating to bankruptcy or
      insolvency in which a debtor's property and affairs are subject to
      control or supervision by a foreign court for the purpose of
      reorganization or liquidation. ([instances
      étrangères]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[foreign representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[foreign representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person or
      body, including one appointed on an interim basis, who is
      authorized, in a foreign proceeding in respect of a debtor, to

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} administer the debtor's property or affairs for
        the purpose of reorganization or liquidation; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} act as a representative in respect of the
        foreign proceeding. ([représentant étranger]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Centre of debtor's main interests

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, in the absence of
  proof to the contrary, a debtor's registered office and, in the case
  of a debtor who is an individual, the debtor's ordinary place of
  residence are deemed to be the centre of the debtor's main interests.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
- 2004, c. 25, s. 102
- 2005, c. 47, s. 122
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-268-20041215.html "Link to previous version of section 268"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Recognition of Foreign Proceeding]{.HTitleText2} {#h-28889 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for recognition of a foreign
proceeding

- **[[269]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-269 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  foreign representative may apply to the court for recognition of the
  foreign proceeding in respect of which he or she is a foreign
  representative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents that must accompany
  application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), the application must be
  accompanied by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a certified copy of the instrument, however
    designated, that commenced the foreign proceeding or a certificate
    from the foreign court affirming the existence of the foreign
    proceeding;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a certified copy of the instrument, however
    designated, authorizing the foreign representative to act in that
    capacity or a certificate from the foreign court affirming the
    foreign representative's authority to act in that capacity; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement identifying all foreign proceedings in
    respect of the debtor that are known to the foreign representative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents may be considered as proof

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The court may, without further proof, accept the
  documents referred to in paragraphs (2)(a) and (b) as evidence that
  the proceeding to which they relate is a foreign proceeding and that
  the applicant is a foreign representative in respect of the foreign
  proceeding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other evidence

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In the absence of the documents referred to in
  paragraphs (2)(a) and (b), the court may accept any other evidence of
  the existence of the foreign proceeding and of the foreign
  representative's authority that it considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Translation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The court may require a translation of any document
  accompanying the application.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
- 2005, c. 47, s. 122
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-269-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 269"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order recognizing foreign proceeding

- **[[270]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-270 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the court is satisfied that the application for the recognition of a
  foreign proceeding relates to a foreign proceeding and that the
  applicant is a foreign representative in respect of that foreign
  proceeding, the court shall make an order recognizing the foreign
  proceeding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Nature of foreign proceeding to be
  specified

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The court shall specify in the order whether the
  foreign proceeding is a foreign main proceeding or a foreign non-main
  proceeding.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
- 2005, c. 47, s. 122
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-270-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 270"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effects of recognition of a foreign main
proceeding

- **[[271]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-271
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) to (4),
  on the making of an order recognizing a foreign proceeding that is
  specified to be a foreign main proceeding,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} no person shall commence or continue any action,
    execution or other proceedings concerning the debtor's property,
    debts, liabilities or obligations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the debtor carries on a business, the debtor
    shall not, outside the ordinary course of the business, sell or
    otherwise dispose of any of the debtor's property in Canada that
    relates to the business and shall not sell or otherwise dispose of
    any other property of the debtor in Canada; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the debtor is an individual, the debtor shall
    not sell or otherwise dispose of any property of the debtor in
    Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When subsection (1) does not apply

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if any proceedings
  under this Act have been commenced in respect of the debtor at the
  time the order recognizing the foreign proceeding is made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The prohibitions in paragraphs (1)(a) and (b) are
  subject to the exceptions specified by the court in the order
  recognizing the foreign proceeding that would apply in Canada had the
  foreign proceeding taken place in Canada under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of this and other Acts

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in subsection (1) precludes the commencement
  or the continuation of proceedings under this Act, the [Companies'
  Creditors Arrangement Act](/eng/acts/C-36) or the [Winding-up and
  Restructuring Act](/eng/acts/W-11) in respect of the debtor.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
- 2005, c. 47, s. 122
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-271-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 271"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Orders

- **[[272]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-272 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  an order recognizing a foreign proceeding is made, the court may, on
  application by the foreign representative who applied for the order,
  if the court is satisfied that it is necessary for the protection of
  the debtor's property or the interests of a creditor or creditors,
  make any order that it considers appropriate, including an order

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign proceeding is a foreign non-main
    proceeding, imposing the prohibitions referred to in paragraphs
    271(1)(a) to (c) and specifying the exceptions to those
    prohibitions, taking subsection 271(3) into account;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the examination of witnesses, the taking
    of evidence or the delivery of information concerning the debtor's
    property, affairs, debts, liabilities and obligations;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} entrusting the administration or realization of all
    or part of the debtor's property located in Canada to the foreign
    representative or to any other person designated by the court; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} appointing a trustee as receiver of all or any part
    of the debtor's property in Canada, for any term that the court
    considers appropriate and directing the receiver to do all or any of
    the following, namely,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to take possession of all or part of the debtor's
      property specified in the appointment and to exercise the control
      over the property and over the debtor's business that the court
      considers appropriate, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to take any other action that the court
      considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If any proceedings under this Act have been commenced
  in respect of the debtor at the time an order recognizing the foreign
  proceeding is made, an order made under subsection (1) must be
  consistent with any order that may be made in any proceedings under
  this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of this and other Acts

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The making of an order under paragraph (1)(a) does
  not preclude the commencement or the continuation of proceedings under
  this Act, the [Companies' Creditors Arrangement Act](/eng/acts/C-36)
  or the [Winding-up and Restructuring Act](/eng/acts/W-11) in respect
  of the debtor.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
- 2005, c. 47, s. 122
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-272-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 272"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms and conditions of orders

**[[273]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-273 .sectionLabel}** An order under this
Part may be made on any terms and conditions that the court considers
appropriate in the circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
- 2005, c. 47, s. 122
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-273-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 273"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commencement or continuation of
proceedings

**[[274]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-274 .sectionLabel}** If an order recognizing
a foreign proceeding is made, the foreign representative may commence or
continue any proceedings under sections 43, 46 to 47.1 and 49 and
subsections 50(1) and 50.4(1) in respect of a debtor as if the foreign
representative were a creditor of the debtor, or the debtor, as the case
may be.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
- 2004, c. 25, s. 103
- 2005, c. 47, s. 122
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-274-20041215.html "Link to previous version of section 274"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Obligations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-28950 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cooperation --- court

- **[[275]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-275 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  an order recognizing a foreign proceeding is made, the court shall
  cooperate, to the maximum extent possible, with the foreign
  representative and the foreign court involved in the foreign
  proceeding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cooperation --- other authorities in
  Canada

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If any proceedings under this Act have been commenced
  in respect of a debtor and an order recognizing a foreign proceeding
  is made in respect of the debtor, every person who exercises any
  powers or performs duties and functions in any proceedings under this
  Act shall cooperate, to the maximum extent possible, with the foreign
  representative and the foreign court involved in the foreign
  proceeding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Forms of cooperation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of this section, cooperation may be
  provided by any appropriate means, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the appointment of a person to act at the direction
    of the court;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the communication of information by any means
    considered appropriate by the court;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the coordination of the administration and
    supervision of the debtor's assets and affairs;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the approval or implementation by courts of
    agreements concerning the coordination of proceedings; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the coordination of concurrent proceedings
    regarding the same debtor.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 12, s. 118
- 2005, c. 47, s. 122
- 2007, c. 36, s. 59
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-275-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 275"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligations of foreign representative

**[[276]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-276 .sectionLabel}** If an order recognizing
a foreign proceeding is made, the foreign representative who applied for
the order shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} without delay, inform the court of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any substantial change in the status of the
    recognized foreign proceeding,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any substantial change in the status of the
    foreign representative's authority to act in that capacity, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any other foreign proceeding in respect of the
    same debtor that becomes known to the foreign representative; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} publish, without delay after the order is made, once
  a week for two consecutive weeks, or as otherwise directed by the
  court, in one or more newspapers in Canada specified by the court, a
  notice containing the prescribed information.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 47, s. 122
:::

### [Multiple Proceedings]{.HTitleText2} {#h-28974 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Concurrent proceedings

**[[277]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-277 .sectionLabel}** If any proceedings
under this Act in respect of a debtor are commenced at any time after an
order recognizing the foreign proceeding is made,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the court shall review any order made under section
  272 and, if it determines that the order is inconsistent with any
  orders made in the proceedings under this Act, the court shall amend
  or revoke the order; and
